_ test_malformed_positive_is_unavailable_in_runner_evidence[False-everyday-/dev/sda] _

monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x72cc7b57c8c0>
tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-365/test_malformed_positive_is_una0')
device = '/dev/sda', method = <MethodId.EVERYDAY: 'everyday'>, large = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('device', DEVICES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize('method', list(MethodId))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize('large', [False, True])
    def test_malformed_positive_is_unavailable_in_runner_evidence(monkeypatch, tmp_path, device, method, large):
        def forbidden(*_a, **_k):
            raise AssertionError('Native process/inventory reached')
        for name in ('subprocess.run', 'subprocess.Popen',
                     'beamo_wipe.nwipe_runner.pinned_nwipe_already_running',
                     'beamo_wipe.nwipe_runner._recheck_identity_under_lock'):
            monkeypatch.setattr(name, forbidden)
        monkeypatch.setattr(runner_module, '_try_log_diag', lambda *_a, **_k: None)
        monkeypatch.setattr('beamo_wipe.evidence.evidence_identity', lambda: {
            'source_commit': 'a' * 40, 'build_id': 'pass15', 'build_status': 'development', 'source_dirty': True,
        })
        discovery = discovery_for_scenario('happy')
        disk = replace(discovery.selectable[0], path=device, name=device.rsplit('/', 1)[-1])
        discovery = replace(discovery, disks=(discovery.boot, disk), selectable=(disk,))
        positive = STAMP + 'No hidden sectors on ' + device + ' -- HIDDEN SECTORS INDETERMINATE!\n'
        padding = ('x' * 65535 + '\n') * 17 if large else ''
        text = positive + padding + runner_module.NWIPE_DRIVE_STATUS_HEADER + '\n' + runner_module.nwipe_status_device_field(device) + ' | Erased | 120MB/s | 00:01:00 | FAKE/DISK\n'
        log = tmp_path / 'fake.log'
        raw = text.encode()
        log.write_bytes(raw)

        class Exited:
            def poll(self):
                return 0

        runner = runner_module.NwipeRunner()
        runner._proc = Exited()
        request = WipeRequest(device, method, discovery.boot.path, str(log))
        result = runner.poll(request)
        assert result is not None and result.ok
        evidence = build_evidence(disk=disk, discovery=discovery, method=method, request=request,
            result=result, started_at_wall=None, ended_at_wall=None, started_mono=0, ended_mono=1,
            argv=[], log_text=runner._log_tail, assessment_log_text=runner._assessment_log_text)
>       assert evidence['checks'][0]['status'] == 'unavailable'
E       AssertionError: assert 'pass' == 'unavailable'
E
E         - unavailable
E         + pass

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("bridge", BRIDGES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ("boot", "mounted"))
    def test_omitted_physical_bridge_keeps_boot_and_mount_protection(fake_machine, kind, bridge, chain, order):

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("chain", tuple(CHAINS))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("bridge", BRIDGES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ("boot", "mounted"))
    def test_omitted_physical_bridge_keeps_boot_and_mount_protection(fake_machine, kind, bridge, chain, order):
        payload, boot_path, bridge_path = _alias_payload(kind, bridge, chain, order)
        result = fake_machine(payload, boot_path)
        assert result.boot_identified and result.boot.path == boot_path and not result.error
        assert boot_path not in {disk.path for disk in result.selectable}
        leaf = next(disk for disk in result.disks if disk.path == "/dev/sdd")
        protected = chain in {"root_missing", "leaf_missing"}
>       assert (leaf.path in {disk.path for disk in result.selectable}) is not protected
E       AssertionError: assert ('/dev/sdd' in {'/dev/sdd'}) is not True
E        +  where '/dev/sdd' = Disk(path='/dev/sdd', name='sdd', model='Fake disk', serial='FAKE-B', size_bytes=128000000000, size_gb_label='128', ki..._only=False, wwn='', vendor='', mountpoints=(), raw_model='Fake disk', contents='unknown', hotplug=False, layout_id='').path

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("bridge", BRIDGES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ("boot", "mounted"))
    def test_omitted_physical_bridge_keeps_boot_and_mount_protection(fake_machine, kind, bridge, chain, order):
        payload, boot_path, bridge_path = _alias_payload(kind, bridge, chain, order)
        result = fake_machine(payload, boot_path)
        assert result.boot_identified and result.boot.path == boot_path and not result.error
        assert boot_path not in {disk.path for disk in result.selectable}
        leaf = next(disk for disk in result.disks if disk.path == "/dev/sdd")
        protected = chain in {"root_missing", "leaf_missing"}
>       assert (leaf.path in {disk.path for disk in result.selectable}) is not protected
E       AssertionError: assert ('/dev/sdd' in {'/dev/sdd'}) is not True
E        +  where '/dev/sdd' = Disk(path='/dev/sdd', name='sdd', model='Fake disk', serial='FAKE-C', size_bytes=128000000000, size_gb_label='128', ki...='50014ee20aa00001', vendor='', mountpoints=(), raw_model='Fake disk', contents='unknown', hotplug=False, layout_id='').path

    def test_omitted_physical_bridge_keeps_boot_and_mount_protection(fake_machine, kind, bridge, chain, order):

        result = fake_machine(payload, boot_path)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("bridge", BRIDGES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ("boot", "mounted"))
    def test_omitted_physical_bridge_keeps_boot_and_mount_protection(fake_machine, kind, bridge, chain, order):
        payload, boot_path, bridge_path = _alias_payload(kind, bridge, chain, order)
        result = fake_machine(payload, boot_path)
        assert result.boot_identified and result.boot.path == boot_path and not result.error
        assert boot_path not in {disk.path for disk in result.selectable}
        leaf = next(disk for disk in result.disks if disk.path == "/dev/sdd")
        protected = chain in {"root_missing", "leaf_missing"}
>       assert (leaf.path in {disk.path for disk in result.selectable}) is not protected
E       AssertionError: assert ('/dev/sdd' in {'/dev/sdd'}) is not True
E        +  where '/dev/sdd' = Disk(path='/dev/sdd', name='sdd', model='Fake disk', serial='FAKE-B', size_bytes=128000000000, size_gb_label='128', ki..._only=False, wwn='', vendor='', mountpoints=(), raw_model='Fake disk', contents='unknown', hotplug=False, layout_id='').path

_ test_final_boot_guard_rechecks_retained_physical_evidence[rom-root_missing-order0] _

fake_machine = <function fake_machine.<locals>.scan at 0x72cc78adf560>
bridge = 'rom', chain = 'root_missing', order = (0, 1, 2)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("order", ORDERS)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("chain", ("root_missing", "leaf_missing"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("bridge", BRIDGES)
    def test_final_boot_guard_rechecks_retained_physical_evidence(fake_machine, bridge, chain, order):
        payload, boot_path, _bridge_path = _alias_payload("boot", bridge, chain, order)
        result = fake_machine(payload, boot_path)
        assert result.boot_identified and not result.error
        leaf = next(disk for disk in result.disks if disk.path == "/dev/sdd")
        reset = replace(leaf, is_boot=False, mountpoints=())
        injected = replace(
            result, disks=tuple(reset if disk.path == reset.path else disk for disk in result.disks),
            selectable=(reset,),
        )
>       with pytest.raises(SafetyError, match="Boot USB appeared"):

_ test_valid_utf8_tail_boundaries_preserve_terminal_roundtrip[U00E9-cut1-everyday] _

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-365/test_valid_utf8_tail_boundarie6')
method = <MethodId.EVERYDAY: 'everyday'>, character = 'é', cut = 1

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("method", list(MethodId))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize(("character", "cut"), _VALID_CASES)
    def test_valid_utf8_tail_boundaries_preserve_terminal_roundtrip(
        tmp_path, method, character, cut
    ):
        first = character.encode("utf-8") + b"\n"
        raw = first + _padding(_RAW_LIMIT + cut - len(first) - len(_STATUS)) + _STATUS
        assert len(raw) == _RAW_LIMIT + cut
        raw.decode("utf-8")  # The complete original log is valid, including boundary failures.
        normalized = raw[-_RAW_LIMIT:].decode("utf-8", errors="replace").encode("utf-8")
        assert (len(normalized) > _RAW_LIMIT) == (cut > 0)
>       _assert_roundtrip(tmp_path, raw, method)

tests/test_utf8_log_snapshot_bounds_pass15.py:130: AssertionError
_ test_valid_utf8_tail_boundaries_preserve_terminal_roundtrip[U1F600-cut1-everyday] _

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-365/test_valid_utf8_tail_boundarie21')
method = <MethodId.EVERYDAY: 'everyday'>, character = '😀', cut = 1

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("method", list(MethodId))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize(("character", "cut"), _VALID_CASES)
    def test_valid_utf8_tail_boundaries_preserve_terminal_roundtrip(
        tmp_path, method, character, cut
    ):
        first = character.encode("utf-8") + b"\n"
        raw = first + _padding(_RAW_LIMIT + cut - len(first) - len(_STATUS)) + _STATUS
        assert len(raw) == _RAW_LIMIT + cut
        raw.decode("utf-8")  # The complete original log is valid, including boundary failures.
        normalized = raw[-_RAW_LIMIT:].decode("utf-8", errors="replace").encode("utf-8")
        assert (len(normalized) > _RAW_LIMIT) == (cut > 0)
>       _assert_roundtrip(tmp_path, raw, method)

___ test_bounded_invalid_utf8_capture_preserves_terminal_roundtrip[everyday] ___

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-365/test_bounded_invalid_utf8_capt0')

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("method", list(MethodId))
    def test_bounded_invalid_utf8_capture_preserves_terminal_roundtrip(tmp_path, method):
        raw = _padding(_RAW_LIMIT - len(_STATUS), b"\xff") + _STATUS
        assert len(raw) == _RAW_LIMIT
        with pytest.raises(UnicodeDecodeError):
            raw.decode("utf-8")
        normalized = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").encode("utf-8")
        assert 2 * _RAW_LIMIT < len(normalized) < 3 * _RAW_LIMIT
>       _assert_roundtrip(tmp_path, raw, method)

        raw = _padding(_RAW_LIMIT - len(_STATUS), b"\xff") + _STATUS
        assert len(raw) == _RAW_LIMIT
        with pytest.raises(UnicodeDecodeError):
            raw.decode("utf-8")
        normalized = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").encode("utf-8")
        assert 2 * _RAW_LIMIT < len(normalized) < 3 * _RAW_LIMIT
